# Public CoD4x capability evidence

Windows custom client/server pair on private localhost/LAN; all capabilities discovered in the pinned public sources remain applicable unless a reviewed exclusion is recorded.

Conservative public C/C++/assembly source census including the SDK union of declarations, P_P_F entrypoints and pluginexports.asm pexport/ralias invocations; comments and literal inactive branches excluded; unknown conditional branches included; dynamic names and opaque registries stay pending. No declaration or stub counts as tested parity.

Release gate: **FAIL**. 2117 capabilities; 0 proven, 2117 pending, 0 reviewed exclusions; 20 dynamic or opaque entries.

A failing gate is expected until applicable behavior has implementation and semantic regression proof. Performance benchmarks alone do not establish CoD4x feature parity.
